  Title  : Maharashtra Board
  URL    : https://allen.in/maharashtra-board/
==============================================================================

Maharashtra State Board of Secondary & Higher Secondary Education (MSBSHSE)
The Maharashtra State Board of Secondary & Higher Secondary Education (MSBSHSE) is the manages school education in Maharashtra, including up to Class 10 (SSC) and Class 12 (HSC). The board sets the syllabus, approves textbooks, and conducts the board exams for Class 10 and Class 12.
This page will help you understand what MSBSHSE does, how your exams work, what you’ll study, and how you can use useful resources to do well.
1.0Quick Facts About MSBSHSE
- Full Name: Maharashtra State Board of Secondary & Higher Secondary Education (MSBSHSE)
- Established Year: January 1 1966 (under the Maharashtra Secondary and Higher Secondary Education Boards Act, 1965)
- Headquarters: Pune, Maharashtra
- Official Website: mahahsscboard.in
- Key Exams: SSC (Class 10) Examination & HSC (Class 12) Examination
2.0History & Background of the MSBSHSE
The MSBSHSE was formed under the Maharashtra Secondary and Higher Secondary Education Boards Act of 1965, and formally started functioning from January 1, 1966.
The board was created to bring standard rules for secondary (10th) and higher secondary (12th) and uniformity in education across the state of Maharashtra. Over time it has grown to cover many schools and helped students finish their Class 10 and Class 12 exams smoothly.
It also has several divisional boards (for Mumbai, Pune, Nagpur, etc.) to manage the large number of students and schools across the state.
3.0Latest Updates / Notifications on the MSBSHSE
- The board has released the exam timetables for both SSC (Class 10) and HSC (Class 12) for February 2026 onwards.
4.0Role & Functions of the Board (MSBSHSE)
- Set and approve curriculum and syllabus for secondary and higher secondary levels.
- Approve and publish textbooks for schools under its jurisdiction.
- Conduct the board examinations (SSC & HSC), fix exam dates, manage results.
- Grant affiliation/recognition to schools and manage standards of education.
- Maintain records, announce results, work on improving quality of education in the state.
5.0Major Examinations Conducted by the MSBSHSE
- SSC (Secondary School Certificate) for Class 10: This exam is conducted yearly for students in Class 10. The exam covers all the prescribed subjects and is usually conducted in the month of February.
- HSC (Higher Secondary Certificate) for Class 12: They are conducted yearly for students in Class 12 in various streams (Science, Commerce, Arts).
- Supplementary/Improvement Exams: This exam is for students who wish to improve their scores or havent passed the examination in the regular attempt. Ths is generally conducted after the declaration of the results in the month of May.
6.0MSBSHSE Syllabus & Textbooks
The subject-wise and chapter-wise details of the curriculum for the schools affiliated with the board is published by the MSBSHSE.
Textbooks are approved by the board and available for different mediums (Marathi/English) depending on your school.
You may consult your teacher or the school for the syllabus PDF and textbook list for your class and medium.
7.0Exam Pattern & Marking Scheme for MSBSHSE
Note: The exact time, marks and question types change year to year. The students must check the latest notification by MSBSHSE for updated details.
8.0Important Dates & Exam Calendar
As mentioned before the MSBSHSE board conducts board exams for the class 10 and Class 12 students. For all the other classes, the exams are conducted by the local schools according to the syllabus prescribed by the board. Below are the exam related dates for the class 10 students:
9.0Eligibility & Application Process
- Regular students: If you are studying in a school affiliated with MSBSHSE, your school will register you for SSC (Class 10) or HSC (Class 12) exams.
- Private / Ex-school candidates: If you are not in regular school or want to appear privately, you’ll need to apply through the board’s process, filling forms, submitting fees and meeting deadlines.
- Make sure your details (name, roll number, school, medium) are correct when applying.
10.0Resources for Students
- Previous Year Question Papers: Practice old exam papers to understand how questions are asked.
- Model Papers/Sample Tests: Solve sample papers as if you are in real exam conditions.
- Answer Keys/Solutions: Use them after solving to check your answers and learn from mistakes.
- Study Tips / Preparation Strategy:
- Make a daily schedule: allocate subject-wise time each day.
- Understand concepts instead of just memorising.
- Solve at least one full paper under timed conditions.
- Revise regularly, especially topics you find difficult.
- Stay healthy: good sleep, nutrition and short breaks help during exam prep.

JEE Advanced Results: How to Check, Rank List, Cutoff & Tie-breaking Policy
For students aiming to secure seats in leading engineering colleges, the annual Joint Entrance Examination (JEE) is a crucial step. Its second stage, JEE (Advanced), is conducted by one of the seven designated zonal IITs under the Joint Admission Board's guidance. JEE poses a significant challenge, requiring students to achieve scores above 90% for admission to esteemed colleges. The conducting authority for JEE Advanced, released the cutoffs and results after each stage. Candidates can access their scorecards on the official website, jeeadv.ac.in, by entering their roll number, date of birth, and registered mobile number. The results include All India Ranks, candidate marks in each subject, final answer key, cut-off, overall aggregate.
Understanding essential details about JEE Advanced results is crucial for candidates. That is why in this page we have come up with all the related information about the JEE Advanced Result.
1.0JEE Advanced Result 2025: Top 10 candidates in CRL list
2.0How to Check JEE Advanced Results?
The JEE Advanced results, including category-wise All India Ranks (AIR) will be announced by organised authorities. Aspiring candidates can access their results on the official JEE (Advanced) website at jeeadv.ac.in.
To see your IIT JEE Advanced results, just follow a few simple steps.
- Visit the JEE Advanced official website.
- Click on the IIT JEE Advanced Result Link.
- Provide your JEE Advanced Roll Number, Date of Birth, and registered phone number.
- Complete the 'I am not a Robot' verification.
- Click 'Submit.'
- Your result will appear on the screen.
- Download the JEE result and print it.
3.0Details Mentioned in the JEE Advanced Result
You should also be aware of the details needed to check the JEE Advanced result. Here, we have mentioned the details that you need to verify in the result.
- Registration number
- Roll number
- Date of birth
- Candidate’s Name
- Qualifying status
- Rank obtained
- AIR (All India Rank)
- Subject-wise marks
- Total marks scored
You can also keep visiting this page for the latest updates about the JEE Advanced Result.
4.0How JEE Advanced Results are Prepared: A Simple Guide
On the basis of last year, we have come up with an expected way in which the results will be prepared this year.
- Marks in Chemistry, Physics, and Mathematics from Paper 1 and Paper 2 are added separately.
- For each subject (Physics, Mathematics, and Chemistry), marks from both papers are combined.
- The average or aggregate marks are then calculated based on the combined subject scores from both papers.
- Ranks are assigned to candidates based on their scores.
- It is compulsory to attempt both papers of JEE Advanced for assessment.
5.0How Will the JEE Advanced Score be Calculated?
Here, we have given the details about the calculation of marks of the JEE Advanced exam
- The JEE (Advanced) exam consists of Paper 1 and Paper 2.
- Each paper has 51 questions, including multiple-choice, integer value, and numerical questions.
- Both papers are worth 180 marks each.
- The total marks for the JEE Advanced exam are 360.
- There are three sections to the exam: Physics, Chemistry, and Mathematics.
- The JEE Adv exam pattern provides insights into the marking scheme and guides candidates on calculating scores.
6.0JEE Advanced Result Rank List
IIT Kanpur has released the JEE Advanced 2025 rank list. Here are some key details about the rank list for the JEE Advanced 2025 exam:
- The rank list is based on the combined marks obtained by candidates in both Paper 1 and Paper 2.
- Separate rank lists for categories like ST, OBC, and SC will be released, considering the specific score relaxations for each category.
- To be included in the rank list, a candidate must pass both papers of the JEE Advanced exam.
- Achieving a high percentile is crucial for securing a place on the merit list, given the challenging nature of the JEE exam that demands significant effort and hard work.
7.0JEE Advanced Result : Tiebreaker Policies
Sometimes, multiple candidates score the same marks, leading to a tie. In such instances, authorities implement tie breaking policies to determine the rankings. Let's explore these tie-breaking rules.[based on the last year trends]
- In case of a tie, the candidates achieving higher positive marks in the JEE Advanced will be awarded a superior rank.
- In case of a tie, candidates with higher marks in Mathematics will be given a higher rank.
- If the tie persists, candidates with higher marks in Physics will be given a superior rank.
- If the tie continues, the committee will assign the same JEE Advanced rank to the candidates.
8.0JEE Advanced Cutoff for 2025
JEE Advanced 2025 cutoff has been released by the IIT Kanpur. Candidates can follow the below details.
JEE Advanced 2025: Qualifying marks
Last year, IIT Madras has released the cutoff of JEE Advanced 2024 after the declaration of the results on 9th June 2024. You can check the JEE Advanced cutoff 2024 below for the preparation of the upcoming exam strategically:
JEE Advanced Cutoff (2024)
9.0JEE Advanced Previous Year Toppers
In the JEE (Advanced) examination, a combined total of 180,372 candidates participated in both Paper 1 and Paper 2. Here are the top ten rankers of the JEE Advanced Result for your reference.
Conclusion:
JEE Advanced is a tough exam, but excelling is key for admission to good colleges and great job placements. If you're unsure where to start or want to crack JEE in one go, join ALLEN. We're the top IIT coaching institute, guiding you for guaranteed success. Best of luck to all candidates!

Madhya Pradesh Board of Secondary Education (MPBSE) – Overview, Exams, Syllabus & Resources
The Madhya Pradesh Board of Secondary Education (MPBSE) is the official state‐board responsible for schooling for Classes up to 10 (and beyond) in the state of Madhya Pradesh. It designs the syllabus, approves textbooks, and arranges major board exams for Class 10 (High School Certificate) and Class 12 (Higher Secondary) students.
This page aims to help you understand what MPBSE does, how your exams work, what you’ll study, and which resources are available to prepare well.
1.0Quick Facts about the MPBSE
- Full Name: Madhya Pradesh Board of Secondary Education (MPBSE)
- Established Year: 1965 (under the Madhya Pradesh Secondary Education Act)
- Headquarters: Bhopal, Madhya Pradesh
- Official Website: mpbse.nic.in
- Key Exams: High School Certificate (Class 10) & Higher Secondary Certificate (Class 12)
2.0History & Background of MPBSE
Through the Madhya Pradesh Secondary Education Act, The MPBSE was established in 1965 to bring uniformity and standardisation to secondary and higher secondary education in the state.
Before its establishment, secondary examinations and related schooling matters in the state were less organised; with MPBSE in place, the board took on the roles of prescribing curriculum, conducting exams, granting school recognition and more. Over time, it has grown to manage thousands of schools across Madhya Pradesh, helping students transition from class to class and preparing for life beyond school.
3.0Latest Updates / Notifications from the MPBSE
- MPBSE has released the exam timetable for 2026: Class 10 exams from February 11 to March 2, 2026, and Class 12 exams from February 7 to March 5, 2026.
- For the academic session 2025-26, online enrolment (classes 9 & 11) deadline extended till October 8, 2025.
- Official website lists various notices: e.g., age-limit relaxation for Class 9 entry, exam fee/deadline changes.
4.0Role & Functions of the MP Board of Secondary Education
The board carries out many important tasks, such as:
- Prescribing the syllabus for secondary and higher secondary classes.
- Approving textbooks and study materials used in affiliated schools.
- Conducting board exams for Class 10 and Class 12, and declaring results.
- Granting recognition/affiliation to schools in Madhya Pradesh.
- Ensuring the board’s policies on teaching standards, building conditions, teacher qualifications are followed.
5.0Major Examinations Conducted by the MPBSE
- High School Certificate (HSC) / Class 10 Examination: These exams are conducted once a year (usually in Feb and Mar).
- Higher Secondary Certificate (HSSC) / Class 12 Examination: Once a year for the higher secondary level (streams like Science, Commerce, Arts).
- Other Exams: The board may conduct examinations for other levels or for special purposes (depending on state policy).
6.0Syllabus & Textbooks Prescribed the MPBSE
The syllabus for classes including 10 and 12, and earlier classes in many cases are published by the MPBSE. So students and schools know what topics will be covered.
Textbooks are either published or approved by the board. Schools distribute these textbooks in the medium of instruction (Hindi/English) depending on the school’s choice.
You can ask your teacher or school office for the latest syllabus PDF and textbook list for your class and medium.
7.0Exam Pattern & Marking Scheme for the MP Board of Secoandary Education
Note: The exact marks, question types and time may change each year. Ensure to always check the latest notification from your school or board.
8.0MPBSE Important Dates & Exam Calendar
MPBSE conducts qualifying exams for classes 10 and 12 on a state level. For all the other classes, the exams are conducted by the respective schools. Below are the tentative dates by the MPBSE for the qualifying exams in the state of Madhya Pradesh.
9.0Eligibility & Application Process
- Regular students: If you are studying in a school affiliated with MPBSE, your school will register you for the board exam when you reach Class 10 or 12.
- Private or ex-school candidates: If you are not in a regular school or want to appear independently, you must check the MPBSE website for application form, fee, deadlines, and follow the process.
- Your details such as name, class, roll number, school, medium must be correct. Pay any required fee and submit the form before the deadline with your school’s help.
10.0Resources by the MPBSE for Students
MPBSE also helps students by proving various resources to aid in the preparation of their exams. Some of which are mentioned below:
- Previous Year Question Papers: Solve past papers to know the pattern and practise time-management.
- Model Papers / Sample Papers: Schools or board may release model papers so you can practise.
- Answer Keys / Solutions: Use them after practising to check your answers and learn from mistakes.
- Study Tips / Preparation Strategy:
- Make a simple daily schedule and stick to it.
- Understand what each topic means, don’t just memorise.
- Take full-length practice tests under timed conditions.
- Focus extra on subjects or topics you find harder.
- Keep yourself healthy, with good sleep, proper meals, and short breaks while studying help.
Frequently Asked Questions

CBSE Syllabus for Class 10
The CBSE has released the updated CBSE syllabus for class 10 on its official website for session 2025-26. Here, we will provide a detailed insight to the students about the CBSE board 10 syllabus. It will help them in their school exams and various Olympiad exams preparation of different levels. Students may go through or download their 10 standard CBSE syllabus for all subjects, which covers the entire curriculum.
1.0Class 10 Subject Selection Scheme
The CBSE class 10 curriculum has been designed as learner-oriented, which will develop a range of skills, not only in key subject areas but also in essential life skills of building self-confidence and developing teamwork & problem-solving skills.It promotes physical fitness, health and arts-integrated learning. To foster these essential skills, the curriculum includes the following different subjects:
2.0CBSE Class 10 All Subject Syllabus 2025-26
Here, a detailed breakdown of the latest CBSE Syllabus Class 10 for the academic year 2025-26 has been provided.
CBSE 10th Mathematics Syllabus
The main topics of the Mathematics syllabus would comprise number sense, geometry, problem-solving, and the development of the ability to think mathematically and apply knowledge in life situations. The course incorporates the teaching of reasoning, recognition, and problem-solving, with Mathematics at both Basic and Standard levels to cater to all types of students.
CBSE Board Class 10 Science Syllabus
The science curriculum focuses on explaining natural phenomena through scientific methods and applying knowledge to improve life experiences. It develops students' ability to engage with science-related issues, design investigations, and interpret data scientifically.
CBSE Class 10 Social Science Syllabus
Social Science is a subject that makes students understand human behaviour and interactions in a cultural, geographical, and historical context. The subject includes history and culture, geography, politics, economy, and civic responsibilities while fostering respect for other people's viewpoints and proper understanding of their Fundamental Rights and Duties.
10th Class CBSE Board Syllabus for Hindi
The 10th class CBSE Hindi syllabus covers Literature through plays, poems, and prose, Grammar with a focus on verb tenses and sentence structure, and Writing Skills that include storytelling, persuasive arguments, and vivid descriptions.
CBSE Class 10 English Syllabus
The 10th CBSE English syllabus comprises Literature, which includes different plays, poems, and prose; grammar, which deals with the formation of sentences and various forms of verbs; and Writing Skills, which involve only narratives, persuasive arguments, and descriptive purposes.

Here's how to get the official study guide in just a few clicks:
Step 1: Go to the CBSE official website.
Step 2: Look for the Curriculum for the Academic Year 2025-26.
Step 3: Click on the tab that says "Secondary Curriculum (IX-X).
Step 4: Click on the DESIRED subject syllabus pdf.
4.0Benefits of Knowing the Class 10 CBSE Board Syllabus
Below is why it will be useful to be aware of the syllabus of CBSE Class 10:
- It gives students an overview of what they will be studying in a particular academic session.
- It properly guides students with their Class 10 Board exam preparation.
- The syllabus of Class 10 CBSE has been structured to effectively provide information on topics ranging from basic to complex.
- Helps students to confidently prepare for their Class 10 Board exams.

English Olympiad
The English Olympiad is a captivating contest that invites students to delve into the richness of the English language and its literature. This competition is designed to nurture competitive spirit, highlight intellectual prowess, and promote excellence in English. With various formats and focuses, the English Olympiads cater to different interests, from creative writing to critical reading and linguistic analysis, depending on the organising body and geographical location.
Membership of the English Olympiad goes beyond the simple sphere of competition. It is an experience of enhancement, learning, and exploration. The platform presents promising writers, keen readers, or prospective linguists with a one-of-a-kind chance to dive into the complexities of English, interact with difficult texts, and bond with other enthusiasts.
1.0IEO 2025 - Important Dates and Events
Candidates looking to participate in the English Olympiad 2025 should refer to the official website for detailed information on exam schedules and critical events.
2.0How to Register for the English Olympiads?
Registering for any English Olympiad involves a few steps, which can vary slightly depending on the competition you're entering. Here's an essential guide designed to navigate you through the registration procedure:
- Identify the Olympiad: Choose the English Olympiad you would like to join. Some options are the International English Olympiad (IEO), TOEFL Challenge, and IELTS Academic Competition.
- Verify Eligibility: Every Olympiad has specific eligibility criteria. Ensure that you or your student is eligible according to the criteria before applying for registration.
- Visit the Official Website: The official website is the main resource for registration details. They present comprehensive registration details, eligibility rules, test structures, and practice advice.
- Fill in the Registration Form: Registration typically entails completing an online form with personal information, educational background, and, in some cases, your preferred test centre.
- Pay the Registration Fee: If there is a registration fee, you will be required to pay it. Payment details and instructions will be made available on the website of the Olympiad.
- Prepare for the Exam: Once registered, begin preparing for the exam. Organisers usually provide study materials, practice questions, and practice exams to assist participants.
3.0List of International Level English Olympiad
4.0Benefits of Participating in English Olympiad Exams 2025
Participating in English Olympiad exams benefits students in numerous ways, enriching both their academic journey and personal growth:
- Enhanced Language Skills: Students deepen their understanding of English, from vocabulary to complex grammatical structures.
- Critical Thinking: Engaging with diverse texts cultivates the ability to analyse, interpret, and critique, fostering critical thinking.
- Creativity: Creative writing sections encourage imaginative thinking and the expressive use of language.
- Confidence and Resilience: Preparation and competition build confidence, while navigating challenges boost resilience.
- Global Perspective: Exposure to literature from different cultures broadens understanding and empathy towards global narratives.
5.0English Olympiad Exam Preparation Tips
Preparing for an English Olympiad can be both challenging and exciting. Below are several strategies to assist you in your preparation journey:
- Understand the Format: Get familiar with the exam pattern, such as the nature of the questions asked, the sections covered, and the time limit for each segment.
- Construct a Good Base: Make sure you have good knowledge of English grammar, vocabulary, reading, and writing skills. These are the basic prerequisites for doing well in any English Olympiad.
- Read Widely: Read different types of texts, fiction, non-fiction, newspapers, and journals. This will enhance your reading speed, understanding, and vocabulary.
- Practice Writing: Most Olympiads have a writing component. Practice writing essays, reports, and letters. Pay attention to structure, clarity, and arguments.
- Use Olympiad Resources: Utilise study guides, sample papers, and previous exam papers made available by the Olympiad organisers. These materials provide insight into the level of difficulty in the exam as well as question patterns.
- Take Part in Language Activities: Join debates, discussions, and other language activities. These can enhance your speaking skills and confidence in speaking English.
- Get Feedback: If you're doing essays or practice tests, ask teachers or classmates for feedback. Constructive criticism can assist you in knowing where you need to improve.
By following these steps and tips, you'll be well on your way to registering for and preparing effectively for an English Olympiad, setting the stage for a rewarding experience that showcases your language skills.

CBSE Class 9 Syllabus 2025-26 – Download PDF for All Subjects
CBSE Class 9 is an introductory year for students getting ready for the board examination of Class 10. It's vital to have a good grip over the CBSE Class 9 syllabus 2025-26 for successful studies. Irrespective of whether you are concentrating on Science, Maths, English, or other topics, having a proper plan of what all subjects will be taught will assist you in planning ahead and studying intelligently.
Here, we will cover the CBSE Class 9 syllabus of all subjects, give chapter-wise divisions, and assist you in accessing the official CBSE Class 9 syllabus PDF. We will also demonstrate how materials such as ALLEN study material and test papers can enhance your preparation.
1.0CBSE Class 9 Syllabus 2025-26 Overview
The CBSE syllabus for Class 9 2025-26 follows the latest NCERT guidelines and is designed to promote conceptual understanding. It is split into Term 1 and Term 2, with internal assessments, periodic tests, and practicals forming part of the evaluation.
This structured syllabus forms the base for Class 10 board exams and competitive exams like NTSE, Olympiads, and even early prep for NEET/JEE.
2.0CBSE Class 9 Syllabus for All Subjects
CBSE Class 9 Syllabus Science
Here’s a chapter-wise breakdown based on the CBSE Class 9 syllabus science 2025-26:
Term 1
- Matter in Our Surroundings
- Is Matter Around Us Pure
- Atoms and Molecules
- The Fundamental Unit of Life
- Tissues
- Motion
- Force and Laws of Motion
Term 2
- Structure of the Atom
- Improvement in Food Resources
- Gravitation
- Work and Energy
- Sound
- Why Do We Fall Ill
- Natural Resources
Use ALLEN Science modules for clear diagrams, simplified concepts, and NCERT-aligned questions to reinforce learning.
CBSE Class 9 Syllabus Maths
Term 1
- Number Systems
- Polynomials
- Coordinate Geometry
- Linear Equations in Two Variables
- Introduction to Euclid’s Geometry
- Lines and Angles
- Triangles
Term 2
- Quadrilaterals
- Areas of Parallelograms and Triangles
- Circles
- Constructions
- Heron’s Formula
- Surface Areas and Volumes
- Statistics
- Probability
ALLEN Maths study material includes step-by-step examples, formula sheets, and chapter-wise practice tests ideal for school and Olympiad-level prep.
CBSE Class 9 Syllabus English
The CBSE Class 9 English syllabus is divided into:
- Reading (comprehension, factual passage, discursive passage)
- Writing and Grammar (paragraph writing, letter writing, tenses, modals, etc.)
- Literature from the NCERT textbooks "Beehive" and "Moments"
Important Chapters (Term-wise):
- The Fun They Had
- The Sound of Music
- The Little Girl
- A Truly Beautiful Mind
- My Childhood
- No Men are Foreign
- The Snake and the Mirror
- Packing
- The Last Leaf
- A House Is Not a Home
ALLEN’s English practice modules include grammar worksheets, comprehension practice, and literature-based sample papers to enhance exam performance.
3.0CBSE Class 9 Syllabus PDF – How to Download
To download the CBSE Class 9 syllabus PDF for 2025-26:
- Visit the CBSE official website: cbseacademic.nic.in
- Navigate to “Curriculum” > “Secondary Curriculum”.
- Click on Class 9 and download the syllabus for each subject.
Alternatively, you can also access updated Class 9 syllabus PDFs via ALLEN’s online platform, where you also get free notes and chapter summaries.

Math Olympiad
Math Olympiads are prestigious competitions that showcase students' mathematical skills and problem-solving abilities. These exams are designed to challenge students intellectually and promote a deeper understanding of mathematics. These olympiads prepare students for future competitive exams and open doors to exciting opportunities in mathematics and related disciplines.
The procedure for participating in Math Olympiads can differ from competition to competition. In general, students have to get themselves registered for the Olympiad exams, usually administered by organisations or educational boards. The process of selection can be more than one round, such as school-level, regional, and national-level tests. Math Olympiads follow the Multiple-Choice Question (MCQ) pattern, which helps candidates think more accurately and cautiously in solving questions.

The Maths Olympiad 2025 Exams are near, and every student is preparing to present their math skills. Bihar School Examination Board (BSEB) – Overview, Exams, Syllabus & Resources
The Bihar School Examination Board (BSEB) is the main state board in Bihar that handles the school education for classes up to Class 10 and Class 12 in the state. The board sets the syllabus, approves textbooks, and arranges the important board exams to assess the students for their further qualification.
This page will help you understand what BSEB does, how their exams work, what the syllabus is and how you can use smart resources to prepare well.
1.0Quick Facts of the BSEB
- Full Name: Bihar School Examination Board (BSEB)
- Established Year: 1952 (under the Bihar School Examination Act)
- Headquarters: Patna, Bihar
- Official Website: biharboardonline.bihar.gov.in / biharboard.net.in
- Key Exams: Matric (Class 10) & Intermediate (Class 12) board exams
2.0History & Background
The BSEB was formed to streamline and manage the board‐level examinations for secondary (Class 10) and senior secondary (Class 12) education in Bihar. Before this, various boards or institutions managed school exams, but to bring uniformity and standardisation across the state, the Board was set up under the 1952 Act. Today, BSEB is responsible for setting the syllabus, issuing textbooks, organising examinations, and declaring results for all schools affiliated with it across the state of Bihar.
3.0Latest Updates / Notifications from BSEB
- Registration begins for BSEB Matric (Class 10) and Intermediate (Class 12) exams for 2025 on September 11, 2024.
- The 2025 exam date sheet: For Matric (Class 10) from February 17 to 25, and for Inter (Class 12) from February 1 to 15.
- BSEB conducts exams in two shifts (morning/afternoon) in many cases.
4.0Role & Functions of the BSEB
- They prescribe the curriculum and syllabus for secondary and senior secondary levels.
- The board conducts the board examinations for Class 10 (Matric) and Class 12 (Intermediate).
- Publishing textbooks or approving textbooks for schools to use.
- Recognising and affiliating schools under its jurisdiction in Bihar.
- Declaring results and maintaining records of student performance.
- Conducting supplementary or improvement exams for students who could not pass in the first attempt.
5.0Major Examinations Conducted by the BSEB
- Matric (Class 10) Examination: This exam is conducted once a year, usually in the month of February, for students in Class 10.
- Intermediate (Class 12) Examination: The intermediate exam is conducted once a year, usually in the month of February, for students in Class 12 across different streams.
- Supplementary/Improvement Exams: These exams are held for students who did not pass in the main exam. This exam is conducted usually in May/June.
6.0Syllabus & Textbooks prescribed by the BSEB
- The Board issues the syllabus for Class 10 and Class 12 so that students know what topics to study. For Class 10 (Matric) you will study languages (first & second), Mathematics, Science, Social Science and an optional subject.
- Textbooks are either published or approved by BSEB and distributed in schools in Hindi, English or regional mediums depending on the school.
- You can ask your teacher or school for the latest textbook list and syllabus PDF for your class and medium.
7.0Exam Pattern & Marking Scheme
Note: The exact marks, question types and time may change each year — always check the latest notification from your school or board.
8.0BSEB Important Dates & Exam Calendar
BSEB conducts exams for class es 10 and 12 on a state level. For all the other classes, the qualifying exams are conducted by the respective schools. Below are the tentative dates by the BSEB for the qualifying exams in the state of Bihar.
9.0Eligibility & Application Process
- Regular students: If you are enrolled in a school affiliated to BSEB, your school will register you for the board exam when you reach Class 10 or Class 12.
- Private / ex-school candidates: If you are not studying in a regular school or have left school and wish to appear, you must apply through the board’s guideline (check the official site for forms, fees and deadlines).
- Make sure your details (name, roll number, class, medium) are correct. Submit the form and fees on time.
- Check with your school for the exact registration process for your session.
Students can directly take admission from any school in the state affiliated with the board during the start of the academic year and their enrolment will be validated directly.
10.0Resources for Students by the BSEB
BSEB also helps students by proving various resources to aid in the preparation of their exams. Some of which are mentioned below:
- Previous Year Question Papers: Solve past exam papers to understand how questions are asked and practise time management.
- Model Papers / Sample Papers: Use model tests to get familiar with exam layout and marking style.
- Answer Keys: After solving papers, use answer keys to check your responses and learn where you can improve.
- Study Tips / Preparation Strategy:
- Make a daily study schedule and stick to it.
- Understand the concepts — don’t just memorise.
- Attempt full-length practice tests under timed conditions.
- Focus more on weaker areas but keep all subjects in practice.
- Stay healthy — good sleep and rest matter before exam days.
Frequently Asked Questions

Indian School Certificate Examinations (ICSE) Class 10 – Everything You Need to Know
ICSE Class 10 Exam Overview
The Council for the Indian School Certificate Examinations (CISCE) conducts the ICSE (Indian Certificate of Secondary Education) examination in India. It is a private board of school education in New Delhi that administers the ICSE (Class 10) and ISC (Class 12) exams. Every year, the ICSE tests are usually held in February or March.
The board is known for its focus on teaching in English and a curriculum that tries to improve students' analytical skills and real-world knowledge.
1.0Key Features & Highlights of ICSE Class 10 Exams
The official ICSE Class 10 timetable for 2026 was released on November 13, 2025, on the CISCE website (cisce.org). The board exams will be conducted in a pen-and-paper format between February 17 and March 30, 2026. Students are advised to check the official portal regularly for any further updates or schedule changes.
Students preparing for the Class 10 ICSE exams 2026 must go through the complete ICSE syllabus 2026 and must know the exam pattern well.
ICSE Exam Pattern and Grading System
The Council of Indian School Certificate Examination (CISCE) put out the Indian Certificate of Secondary Education (ICSE) exam pattern for 2026 on its official website, cisce.org, along with ICSE specimen question papers for 2026.
The ICSE 10th exam pattern for 2026 shows how the questions will be graded, what kinds of questions will be asked, how hard they will be, and other things.
2.0ICSE Class 10 Syllabus & Curriculum Structure
To commence with the preparation for ICSE 10th examinations, one should necessarily go through the complete ICSE syllabus 2026 for various subjects. The board has published the ICSE 10th syllabus 2026 for all on the official website. All the chapters and topics covered in the examination are mentioned in the ICSE syllabus.
Students should try and cover the entire syllabus of ICSE at least a month before the release of the timetable if they want to secure good marks. After finishing the syllabus, the students must also keep time for revision.
The ICSE Class 10 study plan gives a full list of the required subjects and the choices for Groups II and III for the school year. When students know what subjects are on the ICSE 10th grade test, they can choose subjects that interest them.
Group I – Compulsory Subjects
- English
- Second Languages (Indian Languages or Modern Foreign Languages)
- History, Civics, and Geography
Group II – (Any two of the following subjects)
- Mathematics
- Science (Physics, Chemistry, and Biology)
- Economics
- Commercial Studies
- Technical Drawing
- Modern Foreign Language
- Classical Language
- Computer Science
- Environmental Science
- Agricultural Science
Group III – (Any one of the following subjects)
- Computer Applications
- Economic Applications
- Commercial Applications
- Art
- Performing Arts
- Home Science
- Cookery
- Fashion Designing
- Physical Education
- Yoga
- Technical Drawing Applications
- Environmental Applications
- Modern Foreign Languages
3.0ICSE Class 10 Exam Pattern & Grading System
Class 10 Exam Pattern Highlights
The board provides a detailed exam pattern for each subject. Some examples from the board website are mentioned below:
- English I (2 hours): 4 broad questions (composition, letter, unseen passage + grammar)
- Maths (2½ hours): Section A (4 questions of 10 marks each = 40), Section B (solve 4 out of 7 for 40 marks)
- Physics / Chemistry / Biology (1.5 hours each): Section A – short compulsory; Section B – 6 questions, solve any 4 (40 marks)
Grading / Mark Distribution
ICSE Class 10 Study Materials
The learning materials cover content from subjects such as English, Mathematics, Science, and Social Science through the lens of understanding, not memorization. Each topic is deconstructed using examples from real-life scenarios, diagrams and step-by-step solutions to aid comprehension of complex concepts.
At ALLEN, we always ensure that our ICSE study materials are current in accordance with the board and previous exam trends. The materials include mock tests, sample exercises and integrated question banks to aid the student in developing speed, accuracy and confidence.
4.0ICSE Class 10 Specimen Papers- Subject-wise
Practicing ICSE Class 10 Sample Papers/ Specimen Papers (2026) is one of the best and smartest ways to prepare thoroughly for the board examinations. ALLEN provides subject-wise sample papers made by academic experts who create the sample papers based on their observations of the past years’ trends and the latest question patterns of ICSE. These sample papers also replicate exam conditions, which allows students to gauge their preparation level, build their time management, and self-evaluate areas to improve.
Every sample paper has a mix of objective-type, short answer type, and long answer type questions from each of the subjects respective syllabus. The following subjects have separate sample papers: English, Mathematics, Physics, Chemistry, Biology, History & Civics, Geography and Economics. The detailed answer keys and marking schemes explanations help students understand how to present their answers in the board-exam formatting style.
5.0ICSE Class 10 Previous year papers Subject-wise
Previous year question papers for ICSE Class 10 act as good resources to familiarize you with the examination experience. ALLEN classifies their past question papers into subject-wise collections for students to understand the pattern of distribution of questions, repetition of topics, and given marks for the questions. Solving the past five years and up to ten years of question papers, students can easily capture the significance of chapters, question types they are comfortable tackling, and what techniques ICSE is seeking in regard to conceptual knowledge.
By previous year question papers actually asking questions from subjects of the English Language, English Literature, Mathematics, Science (Physics, Chemistry, Biology), Social Science (History, Civics, Geography), etc. Completing these previous year papers in the time frame of the exam helps students gauge their performance, writing exam responses, and then the discipline for the exam.
6.0Admit Card for ICSE Class 10th 2026
The Council for the Indian School Certificate Examination (CISCE) officially released the ICSE Class 10 Admit Card 2026 on January 27, 2026. Please note that the official website (cisce.org) provides download access exclusively to school officials; students cannot download their hall tickets individually.
Students must go to their schools to get their ICSE 10th admit card 2026. You need to have your ICSE admit card 2026 with you on all exam days because it is an important document. Students can't take their tests without it.
7.0Preparation Tips for ICSE Class 10 Students
- Understand the syllabus in depth: The ICSE syllabus is said to be detailed and wide; hence, ensure that you know all the topics.
- Study according to subject-wise patterns: For example, know the pattern for English, Maths, Science etc. as referred above.
- Internal assessment matters: Since internal assessment forms part of the total marks, projects/practicals must not be ignored.
- Practise previous years' question-papers and sample papers: Doing so helps you become familiar with the question-patterns, and timing required to answer the same.
- Time-management: At the time of the exam, take a close look at the marks-weightage; for example, lengthier questions carry more marks.
- Revise systematically: As the syllabus is vast, revision will help retain topics on a weekly or monthly basis.
- Balance between deep understanding and exam strategy: The board emphasizes 'conceptual clarity'; therefore, understand the basics rather than just rote learning.
8.0ICSE Class 10th Result 2026
The Council for the Indian School Certificate Examinations declares results for the class 10th ICSE examination usually in the first week of May 2026. The results will be made available on the official website of CISCE — www.cisce.org.—by entering their unique ID and index number.
- After the declaration of results, original mark sheets and certificates of passing will be issued by the schools, which are necessary for higher secondary admissions.
- After getting their results, students are allowed to select the academic stream of their choice for Classes 11 and 12, such as Science, Commerce, or Humanities, according to their interests and performance.
Students who do not get satisfactory marks, may apply for rechecking or re-evaluation on the official CISCE online portal. The re-evaluation application and result verification window is tentatively scheduled to open in May–June 2026, just after the declaration of results.

ICSE Class 6 Exam Overview
ICSE Class 6 is the foundational stage under the Council for the Indian School Certificate Examinations (CISCE), where students make a smooth shift from primary learning to a more structured middle-school curriculum. Class 6 strengthens core concepts in Maths, Science, English, Social Science, and other important skill-based subjects.
Although the annual examination is conducted internally by each ICSE-affiliated school, all schools follow the syllabus pattern, teaching standards, and academic framework recommended by CISCE.
1.0Key Features & Highlights of ICSE Class 6
2.0ICSE Class 6 Syllabus & Curriculum Structure
The ICSE Class 6 curriculum aims to build a strong academic foundation by introducing structured subject-wise learning. Students begin applying concepts independently and connecting them with real-world situations.
Core Subjects:
- English (Language & Literature)
- Second Language (Hindi, regional language, etc.)
- Mathematics
- Science (Physics, Chemistry & Biology)
- Social Science (History, Civics and Geography)
Optional / Enrichment Subjects:
- Computer Applications
- Environmental Education (EE)
- Moral Science / Value Education
- Art Education
- General Knowledge
- Physical Education
- Performing Arts
Core Syllabus Highlights of ICSE Class 6
Below is a simple subject-wise overview similar to Class 7, but tailored for Class 6 difficulty and topics.
English Language & Literature
Language Skills:
- Basic and intermediate grammar (tenses, prepositions, articles, sentence types)
- Vocabulary building & idioms
- Creative writing (paragraphs, informal letters, story writing)
- Comprehension passages (inference, vocabulary, main idea)
Literature:
- Short stories, poems, and simple drama pieces as prescribed by the school
- Understanding plot, character, theme, and moral values
Mathematics
- Number System (whole numbers, integers – intro, factors & multiples)
- Fractions & Decimals
- Ratio & Proportion (basic)
- Algebra (simple expressions & equations)
- Mensuration (perimeter & area of basic shapes)
- Geometry (lines, angles, triangles, quadrilaterals, circles)
- Data Handling (graphs, tables)
Science (Physics, Chemistry & Biology)
- Physics: Motion, measurement, light, shadows, electricity, magnets
- Chemistry: Sorting materials, changes around us, separation of substances, water, air
- Biology: Food & nutrition, plants, body movements, habitat & adaptations, waste disposal
Social Science (History, Civics & Geography)
History
- Early humans, ancient civilizations
- Early Indian kingdoms
- Culture, society, and community life
Civics
- Understanding diversity
- Our government & local bodies
- Rights, responsibilities & citizenship values
Geography
- Earth as a planet
- Latitudes, longitudes, maps
- Weather, climate & resources
- Landforms and physical features
Computer Applications
- Basics of computers & their components
- Input/output devices
- Introduction to software & hardware
- Word processing basics
- Simple coding concepts (school-dependent)
3.0ICSE Class 6 Exam Pattern & Grading System
Each ICSE Class 6 school prepares its own exam papers, but the structure aligns with CISCE standards.
Theory Exam Format:
- Objective-type questions (MCQs, fill-in-the-blanks, match)
- Short answer questions
- Long, descriptive answers
- Application-based questions in Maths & Science
Internal Assessment Includes
- Class tests
- Homework & assignments
- Projects (Science, Social Studies, Computer)
- Lab/practical activities
- Notebook submission
Passing Criteria: Students must score 30-35% or above in most schools to be promoted to Class 7.
4.0ICSE Class 6 Study Materials & Resources
- Textbooks: Follow the books prescribed by your school. Most ICSE schools use: Selina Publishers (Maths, Science), Morning Star (English Language) and School-selected readers for Literature & Social Science
- Sample Papers & Worksheets: Practising the ICSE Class 6 sample papers helps improve time management, writing skill, and conceptual clarity. Reference Books for class 6 include, Selina Series (Maths & Science), M.L. Aggarwal for Mathematics, Workbook-based grammar practice
- Notes & Revision Guides: Students can maintain self made guides like formula book, diagram notebook, summary notes, etc. to ace their preparation.
5.0ICSE Class 6 Preparation Tips for Students
Below are some of the preparation tips for ICSE Class 6 Students to excel in their examination:
- Build Strong Basics: This is the foundation year. Hence, focus on understanding concepts clearly.
- Maintain Daily Study Time: Study for short intervals daily instead of all at once during exams.
- Practise Writing: English and Social Science answers improve with consistent writing practice.
- Use Visual Tools: Mind maps, diagrams, charts, and tables help in quicker recall.
- Regular Revision: Weekly revision improves long-term memory and reduces exam stress.
- Ask Questions: In case of any doubts, clear them early to avoid confusions later.
- Stay Healthy and Balanced: A good sleep routine, hydration, and break-time help in better learning.
Disclaimer: The information provided above represents a commonly followed structure across many ICSE-affiliated schools. However, the exact syllabus, textbook list, marks distribution, assessment methods, paper pattern, internal assessment activities, weightage, and evaluation criteria may vary from one school to another. Students are strongly advised to verify all details, including syllabus topics, exam format, and prescribed books directly with their teachers or school authorities.
Frequently Asked Questions

CBSE Class 12
Every year, the Central Board of Secondary Education (CBSE) holds the Class 12 board exams. This exam is a significant milestone for students in CBSE-affiliated schools, whether they are studying Science, Commerce, or Humanities. The Class 12 exams are more than just a measure of academic achievement; they are a gateway to your future. Excelling in these exams opens doors to prestigious universities and colleges in India and abroad. To succeed, students must complete both practical and written exams in pen-and-paper mode.
Understanding the full scope of the Class 12 Board Exam is essential before you sit for the exam. This blog covers everything you need to know, from the application process and key dates to eligibility criteria and exam centres. By preparing thoroughly, you can turn your Class 12 experience into a stepping stone toward achieving your dreams.
1.0About CBSE Class 12 Exam
Class 12 exams are a key part of students' academic journey, shaping their future education and career choices. The scores you achieve can be your ticket to prestigious universities and colleges, both in India and abroad. Many institutions use Class 12 results as a crucial admission criterion, significantly influencing your path to a professional degree and future success.
To succeed in these exams, it is essential to choose the right resources and follow a well-planned schedule under the guidance of experts. ALLEN offers various resources for class 12 CBSE subjects like Math, Science, and English to support students. These resources include CBSE solutions, past question papers, sample papers, and syllabus outlines, all created by expert teachers.
2.0Highlights of CBSE Class 12 Exam
Understanding the essential features of the CBSE Class 12 Exam is vital for effective preparation. Below are the major highlights that define this important academic assessment
Students can enhance their preparation for class 12 board exam with the ALLEN class 12 sample papers available at ALLEN estore. This study material aids you in understanding the class 12 exam pattern, marking scheme and the type of questions that can be asked in the exam.
3.0Latest CBSE Class 12 Dates
CBSE releases the much-anticipated date sheet for the 12th board exams every year, typically around November and December. Additionally, CBSE announces the practical exam dates, usually in January. Once the Class 12 written board exams conclude, CBSE promptly releases the final results on its official website.
CBSE ensures that the scheduled dates for these exams are promptly made available to them, and therefore, you can easily access the CBSE date sheet on the official website. The Tentative dates for the upcoming CBSE Class 12 exams (2025-26) were released on 24th SEP 2025.
Details Mentioned in Class 12 Date Sheet
When reviewing the CBSE class 12th board exam date sheet, students need to match and verify the following information carefully:
- Subject Names: Ensure that the names of all subjects are correctly listed on the date sheet.
- Subject Codes: Double-check each subject's codes to avoid confusion.
- Exam Dates: Take note of all the important examination dates for the CBSE Class 12th board exams.
- Exam Guidelines: Familiarize yourself with the exam guidelines specific to each subject.
- Additional Schedule Details: Look for any other relevant details in the exam schedule.
CBSE Class 12th Latest Updates
Here are some of the latest key updates on the Class 12 CBSE Board Exam, as per the officials.
- The officials have released the CBSE class 12th date sheet for the upcoming exam on 24th September 2025
- Keep an eye on the CBSE website or educational platforms for announcements about the 2025-26 academic session.
- Official Class 12 sample papers with solutions are available on the official website. Utilise these sample papers of 2025 for exam format practice.
- Class 12 board exam results for 2025 have been declared. Check the official CBSE website or educational platforms to find out your scores.

Subjects for CBSE Class 12 are generally divided into three streams: Science, Commerce, and Arts. The subjects listed below are ones you should become familiar with in order to prepare well.
5.0CBSE Class 12 Syllabus 2025
The latest circular for CBSE board exams of class 12th will be released at the end of the academic session. Students should follow the most recent syllabus set by CBSE to prepare for this important exam.
There are three main streams in Class XII: Science, Commerce, and Humanities (Arts), and all these three streams have different syllabus. And CBSE Releases the Class 12 syllabus in the following categories:
- Languages (Group-L)
- Academic Electives (Group-A)
- Subjects of Internal Assessment
Please refer to the given link for a detailed overview of the Class 12 syllabus for the academic year 2024-25.
6.0CBSE Class 12th Exam Pattern for 2025
The Central Board of Secondary Education (CBSE) recently introduced updated exam guidelines for Class 12, effective from the 2024-25 session. These new guidelines significantly change the exam pattern, particularly in terms of question weightage.
One notable change is the increased emphasis on competency-based questions. These questions, which include multiple-choice questions (MCQs), case-based scenarios, and source-based inquiries, aim to assess practical knowledge and the application of concepts. The CBSE encourages students to deepen their understanding of subjects and adjust their study techniques through competency-based questions.
We have provided detailed information on the latest Class 12th CBSE Exam Pattern here to provide a clearer picture of the changes that have taken place.

The admit card is an essential document that permits students to appear for the board exams. Approximately one month before the exams commence, CBSE releases the admit cards for class 12 board exams for all students. These admit cards are distributed to students by their respective schools. Students must carefully review their admit cards and ensure that all the information is accurate. The CBSE class 12th admit card contains essential information such as:
- Student's Name
- Roll Number
- Exam Timings
- Exam Center Details
- Date-wise Exam Schedule
- Exam Guidelines
10.0CBSE Class 12th Result 2025
CBSE announced the official Class 12 results on May 13, shortly after the board exams. In 2025, the results were released on May 13. on results.cbse.nic.in, cbseresults.nic.in, and cbse.gov.in. Students can check their results using their hall ticket, roll number, and school number. The results include name of the candidate, roll number, date of birth, grades and subject-wise marks. You can collect the original mark sheets from the school.
After receiving their results, many students explore career options based on their interests and chosen streams. For those aiming for IIT or NEET, ALLEN offers exceptional coaching and guidance to help students excel in competitive exams.

The CBSE Class 12 Board Exam sample papers play a crucial role in effective exam preparation. They are typically released a few months before the main exam and serve as valuable resources.

12.0How to Study for Class 12th CBSE?
To excel in the CBSE Class 12th board exams, focus on effective preparation strategies rather than just studying or memorizing. Here are some concise tips:
Time Management and Revision:
- Plan Ahead: Complete the syllabus early to allow plenty of time for revision and clearing doubts.
- Practice Past Papers: Solve Class 12 the previous years' questions to understand the exam pattern and improve time management.
- Take Notes: Create concise notes for quick revision before exams.
Establish a Routine:
- Create a Schedule: Develop a structured routine and stick to it, ensuring balanced study time for each subject.
Prioritize your Health:
- Get Enough Sleep: Ensure adequate sleep to keep your mind sharp and focused.
- Eat Healthy: Maintain a nutritious diet to support brain function and learning.
Take Breaks:
- Regular Breaks: Include short breaks in your study sessions to prevent fatigue and enhance concentration.

JEE Advanced 2025 Analysis: Paper 1 and Paper 2 detailed analysis
JEE Advanced Examination consists of two papers, which are carried out in two shifts on the same day. The candidates need to appear in both papers to qualify on a merit basis. The online exam is held in various cities across India. JEE Advanced is conducted for the sole purpose of granting admission to the Indian Institutes of Technology (IITs), which are among the most esteemed engineering institutions in India.
In this article, candidates will know the exam pattern, difficulty level, expected cutoff, weightage and marking scheme for the JEE Advanced exam and thus have an idea about their chances of admission. Aspirants can check the detailed JEE Advanced Paper 1 and Paper 2 analysis on this page.
1.0JEE Advanced 2025 Question Paper Analysis: Download PDF & Video
JEE Advanced 2025 was conducted in two sessions (Paper 1 and Paper 2). Paper 1 was conducted from 9:00 am to noon and Paper 2 was conducted from 2:30 pm to 5:30 pm in the evening session for candidates applying for engineering seats in the most esteemed engineering institutions in India. The analysis of the JEE Advanced 2025 question papers will be available here after the exam. Students can check the analysis of Paper 1 and Paper 2, including the marking scheme, difficulty level, types of questions asked, and student reactions. Previous years’ analyses will also be available to help understand the exam trends and difficulty levels over time.
Video Analysis by ALLEN Experts
Below, based on student feedback, we have provided a detailed exam analysis below to help students gauge the difficulty level of the exam and prepare effectively for their next steps.
JEE Advanced 2025 Paper 1 Exam Analysis
Overall, the difficulty level of the exam was moderate to difficult.
Subject wise difficultly level
Physics: Moderate
Chemistry: Easy to Moderate
Mathematics: Difficult
Most students found the JEE Adv 2025 paper 1 was well-balanced. Chemistry was considered the easiest section, while Physics and Mathematics demanded a more thoughtful approach. However, both were manageable within the allotted time, reflecting a fair difficulty level.
JEE Advanced 2025 Paper 2 Exam Analysis
Overall difficulty level: Moderately difficult
JEE Advanced 2025 Paper 2 was conducted on May 18, 2025, in the afternoon session from 2:30 PM to 5:30 PM. The paper consisted of a total of 51 questions, covering Physics, Chemistry, and Mathematics, with five questions in each subject. According to initial student reactions and expert analysis, the overall difficulty level of JEE Advanced 2025 Paper 2 was higher than Paper 1.
Subject wise difficultly level
Mathematics: Mathematics was the toughest section in Paper 2. Questions were lengthy. Questions were asked from the topics: Calculus, Complex Numbers, Circle and Parabola, Probability and Combinations, Vectors and 3D Geometry and Functions and Limits.
Physics: Moderate to High difficulty
Questions were asked from the topics:- Electrostatics and Current Electricity, Thermodynamics and Heat Transfer, Kinematics and Laws of Motion, Ray Optics and Wave Optics and Modern Physics.
Chemistry: The difficulty level ranged from easy to moderate. Compared to Paper 1, this section was more balanced in Paper 2.
Questions were primarily asked from topics:- Electrochemistry, Surface Chemistry, Ionic Equilibrium, Aldehydes and Ketones, Polymers, Biomolecules, p-block Elements, Chemical Bonding, and Coordination Compounds.

In both the papers, questions were asked from Functions, Matrices, Permutation and Combination, Conic Section, Probability and Statics, Complex Numbers, Vectors and 3-D Geometry. The number of questions from Calculus was less as compared to previous years. Solution of the triangle was removed from the syllabus but still, questions were asked by making comprehension.

NEET 2025 Exam Analysis - Exam Over! Live Paper Solutions [Subject-wise]
The country's biggest medical entrance exam, the NEET UG 2025 examination has concluded and was held by the National Testing Agency in an offline pen-and-paper format. The NEET examination consists of 180 questions and the allotted time for the examination is 3 hours. NEET-UG 2025 Examination was conducted in 543 Indian cities and 14 cities abroad. The examination was held in 13 languages: English, Hindi, Bengali, Urdu, Marathi, Assamese, Gujarati, Tamil, Malayalam, Odiya, Kannada, Punjabi, and Telugu.
1.0NEET 2025 Paper Analysis - Video
Biology 2025 Analysis
Physics 2025 Analysis
Chemistry 2025 Analysis
2.0NEET UG 2025 – Paper Analysis by ALLEN
As per ALLEN's experts, NEET (UG) 2025 exam was the Most Difficult in the history of NEET so far. The questions were challenging and asked in a very tricky way.
Subject-wise Paper Analysis - Biology, Physics and Chemistry
NEET 2025 Biology Paper
The paper was very long and complicated. Of the total, 40 questions were from Class 11 and 50 from Class 12.
NEET 2025 Physics Paper
The Physics paper in NEET 2025 was quite difficult and lengthy compared to last year. A total of 22 questions were based on the Class 11 syllabus, while 23 questions were from the Class 12 syllabus.
NEET 2025 Chemistry Paper
Chemistry was very tough and lengthy compared to the last few years.
16 questions were asked from Physical Chemistry, 12 from Inorganic Chemistry, and 17 from Organic Chemistry. Of these, 22 questions were based on the Class 11 and 23 on the Class 12 syllabus.
3.0NEET 2024 Paper Analysis
According to the feedback received from the students and experts, overall difficulty of the NEET-UG 2024 Examination was easy to moderate. The NEET paper was based on the NCERT syllabus. At that time, the Chemistry paper was crucial in determining the rank. No questions were asked from Ionic Equilibrium in Chemistry, and 5 questions came from Physical Chemistry, which had been added to the syllabus that year. However, not a single question was asked about the topic of Experimental Skills, which had also been added to the syllabus. In Biology, some questions were based on old NCERT, while others were from outside the NCERT syllabus.
Subject-Wise Analysis of the NEET-UG 2024 Examination
Physics
- Physics paper was overall tougher than last year. The calculation was difficult for many questions.
- Class-wise, 20 questions were asked from the class 11th syllabus and 30 questions from the class 12th syllabus. Not a single question was asked from the topic Experimental Skills, which was added to the syllabus this year.
- There were 15 questions from Mechanics, 3 from Heat and 2 from SHM and Waves.
- Similarly, 17 questions came from Electrodynamics. Four good-level questions were asked from Optics and nine from Modern Physics and Electronics. Single correct 42, multiple correct 3, one question of assertion and reasoning, two Questions of statement type, and two questions of match the column were asked.
Chemistry
- The Chemistry paper was a little tougher than last year's, but the standard was good overall.
- Inorganic Chemistry was somewhat tougher than last year, Physical Chemistry was tougher than last year, and Organic Chemistry remained the same.
- Class-wise, in class 11th, 12 questions were asked from Physical chemistry, 5 from Inorganic Chemistry and 5 from Organic chemistry. From the 12th class syllabus, there were 7 questions on Physical chemistry, 11 on Inorganic Chemistry and 11 on Organic Chemistry. There were 5 questions from Physical Chemistry, which were added to the syllabus this year. No questions were asked from Ionic Equilibrium. There were 5 questions of statement type and 7 questions of match the column.
Biology
The level of the Biology paper remained the same as last year, and some questions were asked from the old NCERT syllabus, which included topics like animal tissue and cockroaches. Cockroaches were not included in the updated syllabus of NTA, which was added later, and two questions from it appeared in the paper.
Some questions from the NCERT syllabus were categorized as follows:
Botany:
- 27 questions from Class 11
- 23 questions from Class 12
Zoology:
- 27 questions from Class 11
- 23 questions from Class 12
Question Types:
- 45 single correct type questions
- 13 multiple correct questions
- 2 assertion reasoning questions
- 10 statement type questions
- 30 match-the-column questions

2.0Learn English Grammar: Basic to Advanced
Basic Concepts
Start with fundamental topics like parts of speech and sentence structure.

- What is grammar?
- Importance of Grammar in Communication
- Overview of Grammar Rules

- Clauses and Phrases

Advanced Rules
Gradually introduce complex topics like clauses, tenses, and punctuation.

- Punctuation rules (commas, semicolons, etc.).

- Past Tense
- Future Tense
- Common Tense Mistakes
Common Grammar Mistakes
- Subject-Verb Agreement Errors
- Misplaced Modifiers
- Incorrect Pronoun Usage
- Confusing Homophones (e.g., their/there/they're)
Writing Style and Clarity
- Conciseness and Redundancy
- Parallel Structure
- Transition Words
- Tone and Formality
Interactive Exercises
- Quizzes and fill-in-the-blank exercises for practice.

3.0Why should we study English?
The advantages of learning English are many and far-reaching, including
- Global Communication: English is the lingua franca of international communication, allowing you to connect with people worldwide.
- Academic Success English is the language of higher education and research. With the skills in English, you will find your way to succeeding in all academic endeavours.
- Personal Growth: Learning English will enhance your cognitive abilities, improve your critical thinking, and broaden your horizons.
- Cultural Enrichment: Pursuing English literature and culture enriches your experiences by delving into various views, understanding the world's cultures, and appreciating human expression.
4.0Engaging Learning Activities
- Interactive Exercises and Quizzes on Grammar: Test your knowledge of grammar rules through interactive exercises and quizzes.
- Vocabulary Building Word Games: Find words and expand your vocabulary with mind-boggling word games and puzzles.
- Reading Comprehension Passages: Assess your reading skills with a wide range of texts, from short stories to news articles.
- Writing Prompts and Exercises: Move the art of writing forward through answering creative writing prompts and many more exciting writing styles.
- Audio-based Listening Comprehension: Listen and improve. Listen to audio clips and complete interactive activities.
- Track Record: Join other learners and practice your spoken English, where you can practice for more consciousness.
5.0How to master the English language?
The process of learning any new skill requires a lot of practice. Likewise, to become a person who can speak English fluently, you have to learn it right and practise well. Every language is based on four skills—listening, speaking, reading, and writing—collectively called the LSRW skills.
Consistent Practice: Dedicate time to grammar exercises, focusing on sentence structure, verb tenses, and common grammatical errors.
Find Learning Methods You Enjoy: Whether it's through games, songs, or storytelling, make learning fun and engaging.
Expand Your Vocabulary: Learn core vocabulary words and phrases through flashcards, word lists, and reading. Explore diverse genres like novels, short stories, newspapers, and magazines.
Use Mnemonics: Create memory aids like rhymes or visual associations to remember vocabulary and grammar rules.
Consider a Language Tutor: A qualified tutor can provide personalized guidance and feedback.
Utilize Resources: Use textbooks, workbooks, online resources, and reference materials.
6.0How are CBSE English notes helpful for the students?
- Know the grammatical structures & learn new words by practicing them in various exercises.
- Read multiple samples of texts—poems, essays, and stories—to develop reading comprehension and analytical thinking. You can also develop parallel skills to express what you think by using new vocabulary acquired.
- Develop the ability to listen to comprehension and become fluent in spoken English through interactive exercises and games.
- Study English literature; immerse yourself in English literature: read about the development of novel writing and consider examples of this type of writing—poems and poetry readings of the classical kind and works of contemporary poets.

ICSE – Indian Certificate of Secondary Education
1.0Introduction to ICSE
The Indian Certificate of Secondary Education (ICSE) is a well-known school education board in India whose exams are conducted by the Council for the Indian School Certificate Examinations (CISCE), a national-level private board officially established in 1958. Over the years, ICSE has become a popular choice for students who prefer a balanced and detailed curriculum.
With a focus on strong fundamentals, practical learning, and clear understanding of concepts, ICSE gives equal weight to languages, mathematics, sciences, humanities, and skill-based subjects. One of the biggest strengths of ICSE is its emphasis on English. Students learn the language in depth, which later helps in communication, higher studies, and competitive exams.
This page gives you a complete overview of the ICSE board. If you are preparing for exams under the ICSE syllabus, this page will help you plan your preparation effectively.
2.0ICSE Exam
The ICSE exam is the final Class 10 board examination conducted every year by CISCE. These exams are generally held between February and March across India. The detailed timetable will be released in advance by the board so students can prepare effectively. The exam includes a combination of written papers, internal assessments, and project work.
For Classes 6 to 9, the exams are held by the respective schools. Although the board does not directly conduct these exams, schools follow the official CISCE curriculum, textbooks, and guidelines provided. This ensures that students stay aligned with the board’s expectations and are prepared for the Class 10 board exam.

JEE Main Exam Pattern
Joint Entrance Examination (JEE) Main exam is conducted every year by the NTA (National Testing Agency) for admission in B.E, B.Tech, B.Arch, and B.Planning courses. The candidates can get admission to the top institutes across the country. It is also the qualifying exam for JEE (Advanced) exam, which is taken for admission to the prestigious IITs. The JEE Main 2026 exam consists of papers - Paper 1 is for admission to B.Tech/B.E courses, and Paper 2 is for admission in B.Arch/B.Plan courses. The second paper of the JEE Main 2026 will further have two parts - 2A is for B.Arch courses, and 2B is for B.Plan courses.
Students can also benefit from the ALLEN App when preparing for competitive exams such as JEE. Students can download the ALLEN App to make their learning more interactive, immersive, and engaging. Click here to understand the advantages of the ALLEN app.
1.0JEE Main 2026 Exam Pattern
Understanding the JEE examination pattern is important, which is why we have given a detailed account of the JEE Mains pattern 2026.
JEE Main Exam Pattern for Paper-1 (B.Tech/B.E.)
The duration of JEE Main exam Paper 1 is 3 hours (4 hours for the PwD candidates). This paper is held online. There are two sections for each subject - MCQs (Section A) and numerical (Section B). Starting this year, there is a minor change in the paper pattern for each subject (Physics, Chemistry, and Mathematics): Section A will have 20 questions, and Section B will have 5 questions, with all 75 questions being compulsory. (based on previous year details)
The exam is conducted to judge the theoretical and practical knowledge of the students. Let us now discuss the marking scheme of the exam.
JEE Main Exam Pattern for Paper-2 (B.Arch and B.Plan.)
JEE Main exam paper pattern for Paper 2 is different for B.Arch and B.Planning. However, candidates get 3 hours for both these papers.
For B.Arch (Paper 2A):- The exam for B.Arch is held in online and offline mode. The Aptitude and Mathematics test is conducted in online mode, and the Art or Structure Drawing test is held in pen and paper or offline mode.
- Only 2 questions are asked from the drawing section, which assesses the drawing skills of the candidates.
- 20 MCQs (in Section -A) and 5 numerical questions (in Section-B) are asked in the Mathematics section. All questions are compulsory.
- 50 questions are asked in the Aptitude section.
- Total Number of Questions: 77
For B.Plan (Paper 2B):- The exam for B.Arch is conducted in online mode. Questions are asked from 3 subjects, namely Planning, Mathematics, and Aptitude.
- 25 questions are asked in the planning section.
- 20 MCQs and 5 numerical questions are asked in the Mathematics section.
- 50 questions are asked in the Aptitude section.
Here is the table for a better understanding of the Paper-2 Pattern:
Particulars | Details |
Duration | 3 Hours |
Mode of exam | Paper 2A: Online and Offline Paper 2B: Online |
Number Of Sections | Paper 2A: 1. Mathematics 2. Aptitude test 3. Drawing test Paper 2B: 1. Mathematics 2. Aptitude test 3. Planning test |
Total number of questions | Paper 2A: 77 Questions Paper 2B: 100Questions |
Total marks | 400 marks for each paper |
Type of Questions | Paper 2A: Multiple choice questions, Numerical based questions, Drawing Aptitude Paper 2B: Multiple choice and Numerical based questions |
Marking System | Paper 2A: +4 marks for each correct answer, -1 marks for each wrong answer, and 0 marks for each unattempted answer. For the Drawing Test, 50 marks for each of the 2 questions |
Languages | English, Hindi, Assamese, Bengali, Kannada, Malayalam, Marathi, Odia, Punjabi, Tamil, Telugu, Urdu and Gujarati |
2.0JEE Paper Pattern with Marking Scheme
Candidates can find the marking scheme for all the papers of the JEE Main below.
Paper 1 (B.E./B.Tech)
- The total marks for JEE Main exam Paper 1 are 300. For every correct response, 4 marks are rewarded.
- For every wrong response, 1 mark is deducted. No marks are deducted for unattempted questions.
- In the Mathematics, Physics, and Chemistry sections, a maximum of 100 marks are rewarded for all 25 questions. There are a total of 75 questions to be attempted, which equals 75*4 = 300 marks.
Paper 2 (B.Arch/B.Planning)
- The total marks for JEE Main exam Paper 2 are 800, which means 400 marks each for B.Arch (2A) and B.Plan (2B).
- For every correct response, 4 marks are rewarded. For every wrong response, 1 mark is deducted. No marks are deducted for unattempted questions.
Paper 2A (B.Arch)
- In the drawing section, a maximum of 50 marks are awarded for each of the 2 drawing questions- which equals 100 marks.
- In the Mathematics section, a maximum of 4 marks are awarded for each of the 25 questions to be attempted, which equals 100 marks.
- In the Aptitude section, 4 marks are awarded for each of the 50 questions - a maximum of 200 marks.
Paper 2B (B.Plan)
- In the planning-based section, 4 marks are awarded for each of the 25 questions - which equals 100 marks.
- In the Mathematics section, a maximum of 4 marks are rewarded for each of the 25 questions - which equals 100 marks.
- In the Aptitude section, 4 marks are rewarded for each of the 50 questions- a maximum of 200 marks.

NTA JEE Main 2026 Results OUT
Latest Update: JEE Main 2026 Session 1 Result OUT. The National Testing Agency (NTA) has released the JEE Main 2026 Session 1 result for Paper 1 and Paper 2 on the official websites: jeemain.nta.nic.in.
NTA JEE Main Results 2026: The official website of the National Testing Agency (NTA) is the designated platform for releasing JEE Main results. The results for JEE Mains 2026 session 1 Paper 1 (B.E./B.Tech) released on 16th February and for Paper 2 (B.Arch and B.Planing) is released on 24th February 2026. JEE Main Session 2 and Paper 2 (B.Arch/B.Planning) will be released tentatively by 19th April 2026. Candidates can check the official JEE Main website (jeemain.nta.nic.in) for the latest updates and to download their results once it is announced.
A user-friendly, step-by-step guide for checking and downloading results is available on this page, ensuring a smooth and straightforward process for the candidates. Candidates can download the JEE Main answer key and find the expected relationship between JEE Main marks, rank, and JEE Main 2026 percentile based on their scores in JEE Main 2026. Also, aspirants can calculate their percentile based on marks scored using JEE Main 2026 percentile predictor.
